## Latest C10014 NRS 484B.477(2) construction, 2026-11-05

Started the next incomplete assignment from its exact local statute: a person shall not stop, stand or park within an alley in a position that blocks an abutting property's driveway or entrance. The current two unselected case definitions compare an alley stop without and with geometric entrance overlap; the parked-vehicle and broader statutory branches have not been closed. ### Priority C329610 NRS 484B.400(2)(a) Native orientation review, 2026-11-06

**Correction to this historical checkpoint:** The earlier negative-y Native-survey conclusion in this section was wrong. CARLA's published OpenDRIVE lane transform explicitly flips waypoint y (`dp.location.y *= -1`) in [Lane.cpp](https://carla.org/Doxygen/html/dc/d13/Lane_8cpp_source.html). Authored OpenDRIVE approach lanes at negative y therefore correspond to positive-y CARLA Native lanes; the authored northbound exit corresponds to a southbound Native exit. The earlier test pass only established consistency with the mistaken fixture. The current code and checks restore the reflected Native survey, spawn, controller route, full-body exit and saved fixtures. Current hashes and proof scope are stated in the latest continuation below. No CARLA run was made.

CARLA’s published [Lane.cpp transform](https://carla.org/Doxygen/html/dc/d13/Lane_8cpp_source.html) flips OpenDRIVE y when computing Native lane waypoints. I restored the C329610 (2)(a) controller route to `(x, -y)`, surveyed the positive-y eastbound approach and southbound Native exit, spawned at reflected y, and aligned the right-half and full-body exit checks.
